#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Types to represent unstructured mesh elements
using idx_t = uint64_t;
using val_t = double;

// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)
constexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;

// Material properties for energy transfer
struct Material {
    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient
    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink
};

// Static connectivity information for each element
struct ElementStatic {
    idx_t material_idx;
    idx_t num_connections;
    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements
    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection
};

// Dynamic state for each element
struct ElementDynamic {
    val_t current_energy;
    val_t total_flux;
};

// World state
struct World {
    std::vector<Material> materials;
    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;
    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;
    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;
};

// Material type IDs
constexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;
constexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;
constexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;

// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh
// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries
void buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {
    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;
    
    // Initialize materials
    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material
    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material
    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material
    
    // Allocate elements
    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);
    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);
    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);
    
    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy
    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {
        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;
        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;
        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;
    }
    
    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid
    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {
        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {
            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;
            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];
            
            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)
            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};
            
            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {
                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];
                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];
                
                // Check if neighbor is within bounds
                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {
                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;
                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;
                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;
                    elem.num_connections++;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics
    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
}

// Compute energy flux between two elements
inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,
                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {
    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * 
           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;
}

// Run simulation for n_iters iterations
void runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
        // Update all elements
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {
            const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];
            const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[i];
            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];
            
            // Start with external flow
            val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
            
            // Add flux from all connected elements
            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {
                const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];
                const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[neighbor_idx];
                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);
            }
            
            // Update element state
            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];
            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;
            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);
        }
        
        // Swap buffers
        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
    }
}

// Validate simulation results
bool validateResults(const World& world) {
    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
    
    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {
        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;
        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;
        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);
        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);
    }
    
    printf("Validation results:\n");
    printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
    printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
    printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
    
    // Check for numerical issues
    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;
    
    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
        printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {
        printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows
    }
    
    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
        printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
        printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
    return true;
}

// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification
uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {
    uint64_t hash = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {
        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values
        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);
        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);
        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
    }
    return hash;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

fatal: not a git repository (or any parent up to mount point /)
Stopping at filesystem boundary (GIT_DISCOVERY_ACROSS_FILESYSTEM not set).

codex
The mesh has a regular row structure beneath its unstructured connectivity lists, so I’m using a row-block ownership model: each rank stores only its owned elements plus two energy halo rows. Each iteration will post nonblocking nearest-neighbor exchanges, compute communication-independent rows while messages are in flight, then finish boundary rows. Final hashing remains distributed; full result gathering happens only when `-r` explicitly requests global output.

+static inline void updateRows(World& world, int begin_row, int end_row) {

+            world.energy_swap[i] = this_energy + flux;
+            world.total_flux_swap[i] = world.total_flux[i] + std::abs(flux);
+        }
+    }

+static inline void updateRows(World& world, int begin_row, int end_row) {

+            world.total_flux_swap[i] = world.total_flux[i] + std::abs(flux);
+        }

+static void rowPartition(int n, int size, int rank, int& first, int& count) {
+    const int base = n / size;
+    const int remainder = n % size;
+    count = base + (rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    first = rank * base + std::min(rank, remainder);
+}

+static inline void updateRows(World& world, int begin_row, int end_row) {
+    const int n = world.grid_size;

+        const size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(local_x) * n;
+        const size_t end = begin + n;

+            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem.material_idx];
+            const val_t this_energy = world.energy[i];
+            val_t flux = mat.external_flow;

+                                    world.energy[elem.connected_idx[j]]);
+            }
+            world.energy_swap[i] = this_energy + flux;
+            world.total_flux_swap[i] = world.total_flux[i] + std::abs(flux);
+        }
+    }

+static void rowPartition(int n, int size, int rank, int& first, int& count) {
+    const int base = n / size;
+    const int remainder = n % size;
+    count = base + (rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    first = rank * base + std::min(rank, remainder);
+}

+void buildSquare2D(World& world, int n, int rank, int size) {

+    const size_t halo = world.local_rows == 0 ? 0 : static_cast<size_t>(2) * n;
+    world.elements_static.resize(owned);

+    world.energy_swap.resize(owned + halo);
+    world.total_flux.assign(owned, 0.0);

+    const idx_t next_halo = owned + n;

+        const int x = world.first_row + local_x;

+            if ((x == 0 && y == 0) || (x == last && y == last)) {
+                elem.material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
+            } else if ((x == 0 && y == last) || (x == last && y == 0)) {
+                elem.material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+            }
+
+            // Preserve the original connection and floating-point evaluation order:
+            // x+1, x-1, y+1, y-1.
+            if (x + 1 < n) {
+                elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] =
+                    local_x + 1 < world.local_rows ? local_idx + n : next_halo + y;
+                elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections++] = 1.0;
+            }
+            if (x > 0) {
+                elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] =

+                elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections++] = 1.0;
+            }
+            if (y + 1 < n) {

+                elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections++] = 1.0;
+            }
+            if (y > 0) {
+                elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = local_idx - 1;
+                elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections++] = 1.0;
             }
         }
     }

-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
-    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;

+static inline void updateRows(World& world, int begin_row, int end_row) {
+    const int n = world.grid_size;
+    for (int local_x = begin_row; local_x < end_row; ++local_x) {
+        const size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(local_x) * n;
+        const size_t end = begin + n;
+        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {
+            const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];
+            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem.material_idx];
+            const val_t this_energy = world.energy[i];
+            val_t flux = mat.external_flow;
+            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem.num_connections; ++j) {
+                flux += computeFlux(mat, this_energy, elem.connected_flux[j],
+                                    world.energy[elem.connected_idx[j]]);
+            }
+            world.energy_swap[i] = this_energy + flux;
+            world.total_flux_swap[i] = world.total_flux[i] + std::abs(flux);
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+static inline void updateRows(World& world, int begin_row, int end_row) {

+            world.total_flux_swap[i] = world.total_flux[i] + std::abs(flux);
+        }
+    }

+    return (other_energy - this_energy) * mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;
 }
 
-// Run simulation for n_iters iterations
-void runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
-    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();
-    
+static inline void updateRows(World& world, int begin_row, int end_row) {
+    const int n = world.grid_size;
+    for (int local_x = begin_row; local_x < end_row; ++local_x) {
+        const size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(local_x) * n;
+        const size_t end = begin + n;
+        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {
+            const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];
+            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem.material_idx];
+            const val_t this_energy = world.energy[i];
+            val_t flux = mat.external_flow;
+            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem.num_connections; ++j) {
+                flux += computeFlux(mat, this_energy, elem.connected_flux[j],
+                                    world.energy[elem.connected_idx[j]]);
+            }
+            world.energy_swap[i] = this_energy + flux;
+            world.total_flux_swap[i] = world.total_flux[i] + std::abs(flux);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void runSimulation(World& world, int n_iters) {
+    const int n = world.grid_size;
+    const size_t owned = world.elements_static.size();

-                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);
+        MPI_Request requests[4];
+        int request_count = 0;
+        if (world.local_rows != 0) {
+            if (world.previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+                MPI_Irecv(world.energy.data() + owned, n, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                          world.previous_rank, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]);

+            if (world.next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+                MPI_Irecv(world.energy.data() + owned + n, n, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                          world.next_rank, 100, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]);
+                MPI_Isend(world.energy.data() + owned - n, n, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                          world.next_rank, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]);
+            }

+            // Interior rows have no dependency on halo traffic.
+            if (world.local_rows > 2) {
+                updateRows(world, 1, world.local_rows - 1);
+            }
+            if (request_count != 0) {
+                MPI_Waitall(request_count, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+            }
+            updateRows(world, 0, 1);

+        std::swap(world.energy, world.energy_swap);
+        std::swap(world.total_flux, world.total_flux_swap);
     }

+static void rowPartition(int n, int size, int rank, int& first, int& count) {
+    const int base = n / size;
+    const int remainder = n % size;
+    count = base + (rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    first = rank * base + std::min(rank, remainder);
+}
+
+void buildSquare2D(World& world, int n, int rank, int size) {
+    world.grid_size = n;
+    rowPartition(n, size, rank, world.first_row, world.local_rows);

+    world.total_flux.assign(owned, 0.0);

+    const idx_t previous_halo = owned;
+    const idx_t next_halo = owned + n;
+    const int last = n - 1;

+    for (int local_x = 0; local_x < world.local_rows; ++local_x) {
+        const int x = world.first_row + local_x;
+        for (int y = 0; y < n; ++y) {

+            } else if ((x == 0 && y == last) || (x == last && y == 0)) {
+                elem.material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
+            }
+
+            // Preserve the original connection and floating-point evaluation order:
+            // x+1, x-1, y+1, y-1.
+            if (x + 1 < n) {

+static inline void updateRows(World& world, int begin_row, int end_row) {
+    const int n = world.grid_size;
+    for (int local_x = begin_row; local_x < end_row; ++local_x) {
+        const size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(local_x) * n;
+        const size_t end = begin + n;
+        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {
+            const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];
+            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem.material_idx];
+            const val_t this_energy = world.energy[i];
+            val_t flux = mat.external_flow;
+            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem.num_connections; ++j) {
+                flux += computeFlux(mat, this_energy, elem.connected_flux[j],
+                                    world.energy[elem.connected_idx[j]]);
+            }
+            world.energy_swap[i] = this_energy + flux;
+            world.total_flux_swap[i] = world.total_flux[i] + std::abs(flux);
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+    return rank == 0 ? global_hash : 0;
 }

    50	constexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;
    51	
    52	static void rowPartition(int n, int size, int rank, int& first, int& count) {
    53	    const int base = n / size;
    54	    const int remainder = n % size;
    55	    count = base + (rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);
    56	    first = rank * base + std::min(rank, remainder);
    57	}
    58	
    59	void buildSquare2D(World& world, int n, int rank, int size) {
    60	    world.grid_size = n;
    61	    rowPartition(n, size, rank, world.first_row, world.local_rows);
    62	    if (world.local_rows != 0) {
    63	        world.previous_rank = world.first_row == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
    64	        world.next_rank = world.first_row + world.local_rows == n ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
    65	    }
    66	
    67	    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});
    68	    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});
    69	    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});
    70	
    71	    const size_t owned = static_cast<size_t>(world.local_rows) * n;
    72	    const size_t halo = world.local_rows == 0 ? 0 : static_cast<size_t>(2) * n;
    73	    world.elements_static.resize(owned);
    74	    world.energy.assign(owned + halo, 0.0);
    75	    world.energy_swap.resize(owned + halo);
    76	    world.total_flux.assign(owned, 0.0);
    77	    world.total_flux_swap.resize(owned);
    78	
    79	    const idx_t previous_halo = owned;
    80	    const idx_t next_halo = owned + n;
    81	    const int last = n - 1;
    82	
    83	    for (int local_x = 0; local_x < world.local_rows; ++local_x) {
    84	        const int x = world.first_row + local_x;
    85	        for (int y = 0; y < n; ++y) {
    86	            const size_t local_idx = static_cast<size_t>(local_x) * n + y;
    87	            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[local_idx];
    88	            elem.material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
    89	            elem.num_connections = 0;
    90	
    91	            if ((x == 0 && y == 0) || (x == last && y == last)) {
    92	                elem.material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
    93	            } else if ((x == 0 && y == last) || (x == last && y == 0)) {
    94	                elem.material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
    95	            }
    96	
    97	            // Preserve the original connection and floating-point evaluation order:
    98	            // x+1, x-1, y+1, y-1.
    99	            if (x + 1 < n) {
   100	                elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] =
   101	                    local_x + 1 < world.local_rows ? local_idx + n : next_halo + y;
   102	                elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections++] = 1.0;
   103	            }
   104	            if (x > 0) {
   105	                elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] =
   106	                    local_x > 0 ? local_idx - n : previous_halo + y;
   107	                elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections++] = 1.0;
   108	            }
   109	            if (y + 1 < n) {
   110	                elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = local_idx + 1;
   111	                elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections++] = 1.0;
   112	            }
   113	            if (y > 0) {
   114	                elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = local_idx - 1;
   115	                elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections++] = 1.0;
   116	            }
   117	        }
   118	    }
   119	}
   120	
   121	inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, val_t this_energy,
   122	                         val_t connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {
   123	    return (other_energy - this_energy) * mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;
   124	}
   125	
   126	static inline void updateRows(World& world, int begin_row, int end_row) {
   127	    const int n = world.grid_size;
   128	    for (int local_x = begin_row; local_x < end_row; ++local_x) {
   129	        const size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(local_x) * n;
   130	        const size_t end = begin + n;
   131	        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {
   132	            const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];
   133	            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem.material_idx];
   134	            const val_t this_energy = world.energy[i];
   135	            val_t flux = mat.external_flow;
   136	            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem.num_connections; ++j) {
   137	                flux += computeFlux(mat, this_energy, elem.connected_flux[j],
   138	                                    world.energy[elem.connected_idx[j]]);
   139	            }
   140	            world.energy_swap[i] = this_energy + flux;
   141	            world.total_flux_swap[i] = world.total_flux[i] + std::abs(flux);
   142	        }
   143	    }
   144	}
   145	
   146	void runSimulation(World& world, int n_iters) {
   147	    const int n = world.grid_size;
   148	    const size_t owned = world.elements_static.size();
   149	
   150	    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
   151	        MPI_Request requests[4];
   152	        int request_count = 0;
   153	        if (world.local_rows != 0) {
   154	            if (world.previous_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   155	                MPI_Irecv(world.energy.data() + owned, n, MPI_DOUBLE,
   156	                          world.previous_rank, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]);
   157	                MPI_Isend(world.energy.data(), n, MPI_DOUBLE,
   158	                          world.previous_rank, 100, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]);
   159	            }
   160	            if (world.next_rank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   161	                MPI_Irecv(world.energy.data() + owned + n, n, MPI_DOUBLE,
   162	                          world.next_rank, 100, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]);
   163	                MPI_Isend(world.energy.data() + owned - n, n, MPI_DOUBLE,
   164	                          world.next_rank, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]);
   165	            }
   166	
   167	            // Interior rows have no dependency on halo traffic.
   168	            if (world.local_rows > 2) {
   169	                updateRows(world, 1, world.local_rows - 1);
   170	            }
   171	            if (request_count != 0) {
   172	                MPI_Waitall(request_count, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
   173	            }
   174	            updateRows(world, 0, 1);
   175	            if (world.local_rows > 1) {
   176	                updateRows(world, world.local_rows - 1, world.local_rows);
   177	            }
   178	        }
   179	        std::swap(world.energy, world.energy_swap);
   180	        std::swap(world.total_flux, world.total_flux_swap);
   181	    }
   182	}
   183	
   184	bool validateResults(const World& world, int rank) {
   185	    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;
   186	    val_t local_flux_sum = 0.0;
   187	    val_t local_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
   188	    val_t local_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   189	    for (size_t i = 0; i < world.elements_static.size(); ++i) {
   190	        local_energy_sum += world.energy[i];
   191	        local_flux_sum += world.total_flux[i];
   192	        local_max = std::max(local_max, world.energy[i]);
   193	        local_min = std::min(local_min, world.energy[i]);
   194	    }
   195	
   196	    val_t energy_sum = 0.0, flux_sum = 0.0;
   197	    val_t energy_max = 0.0, energy_min = 0.0;
   198	    MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   199	    MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   200	    MPI_Reduce(&local_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   201	    MPI_Reduce(&local_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   202	
   203	    int valid = 1;
   204	    if (rank == 0) {
   205	        std::printf("Validation results:\n");
   206	        std::printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
   207	        std::printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
   208	        std::printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
   209	        if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
   210	            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
   211	            valid = 0;
   212	        }
   213	        if (std::abs(energy_sum) > 1e-8) {
   214	            std::printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
   215	        }
   216	        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
   217	            std::printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
   218	            valid = 0;
   219	        }
   220	        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
   221	            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
   222	            valid = 0;
   223	        }
   224	        if (valid) std::printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
   225	    }
   226	    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   227	    return valid != 0;
   228	}
   229	
   230	uint64_t computeDistributedHash(const World& world, int rank) {
   231	    uint64_t local_hash = 0;
   232	    const uint64_t global_offset = static_cast<uint64_t>(world.first_row) * world.grid_size;
   233	    for (size_t i = 0; i < world.elements_static.size(); ++i) {
   234	        uint64_t energy_bits, flux_bits;
   235	        std::memcpy(&energy_bits, &world.energy[i], sizeof(energy_bits));
   236	        std::memcpy(&flux_bits, &world.total_flux[i], sizeof(flux_bits));
   237	        const uint64_t global_i = global_offset + i;
   238	        local_hash ^= (energy_bits + global_i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
   239	        local_hash ^= (flux_bits + global_i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
   240	    }
   241	    uint64_t global_hash = 0;
   242	    MPI_Reduce(&local_hash, &global_hash, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_BXOR, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   243	    return rank == 0 ? global_hash : 0;
   244	}
   245	
   246	void printUsage(const char* program) {
   247	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", program);
   248	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   249	    std::printf("  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\n");
   250	    std::printf("  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\n");
   251	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   252	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   253	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   254	}
   255	
   256	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   257	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   258	    int rank, size;
   259	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   260	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);
   261	
   262	    int n = 512;
   263	    int n_iters = 10;
   264	    bool validate = false;
   265	    bool printResults = false;
   266	    int parse_status = 0;
   267	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   268	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) n = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
   269	        else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) n_iters = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
   270	        else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) validate = true;
   271	        else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) printResults = true;
   272	        else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) parse_status = 2;
   273	        else parse_status = 1;
   274	    }
   275	    if (parse_status != 0 || n <= 0 || n_iters < 0) {
   276	        if (rank == 0) {
   277	            if (parse_status == 1) std::printf("Unknown or incomplete option\n");
   278	            else if (parse_status == 0) std::printf("Grid size must be positive and iterations nonnegative\n");
   279	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   280	        }
   281	        MPI_Finalize();
   282	        return parse_status == 2 ? 0 : 1;
   283	    }
   284	
   285	    const uint64_t n_elements = static_cast<uint64_t>(n) * n;
   286	    if (rank == 0) {
   287	        std::printf("Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\n");
   288	        std::printf("============================================\n");
   289	        std::printf("Grid size: %d x %d = %llu elements\n", n, n,
   290	                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(n_elements));
   291	        std::printf("Iterations: %d\n", n_iters);
   292	        std::printf("MPI ranks: %d\n", size);
   293	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   294	        std::printf("Building unstructured mesh...\n");
   295	    }
   296	
   297	    World world;
   298	    buildSquare2D(world, n, rank, size);
   299	    const uint64_t static_mem = n_elements * sizeof(ElementStatic);
   300	    const uint64_t dynamic_mem = n_elements * sizeof(val_t) * 4;
   301	    if (rank == 0) {
   302	        std::printf("Memory usage: %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\n\n",
   303	                    (static_mem + dynamic_mem) / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
   304	                    static_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
   305	                    dynamic_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
   306	        std::printf("Running simulation...\n");
   307	    }
   308	
   309	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   310	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   311	    runSimulation(world, n_iters);
   312	    const double local_seconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   313	    double seconds = 0.0;
   314	    MPI_Reduce(&local_seconds, &seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   315	
   316	    const uint64_t result_hash = computeDistributedHash(world, rank);
   317	    if (rank == 0) {
   318	        const double duration_ms = seconds * 1000.0;
   319	        const int measured_iters = std::max(n_iters - 1, 1);
   320	        const double time_per_iter = duration_ms / measured_iters;
   321	        const double giga_elements_per_second = seconds > 0.0
   322	            ? (static_cast<double>(measured_iters) * n_elements) / seconds / 1e9 : 0.0;
   323	        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration_ms);
   324	        std::printf("Performance:\n");
   325	        std::printf("  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\n", time_per_iter);
   326	        std::printf("  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\n", giga_elements_per_second);
   327	        std::printf("  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\n", giga_elements_per_second * 22.0);
   328	        std::printf("  Result hash: %016llX\n\n",
   329	                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(result_hash));
   330	    }
   331	
   332	    if (printResults) {
   333	        std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
   334	        std::vector<double> global_energy;
   335	        const int local_count = static_cast<int>(world.elements_static.size());
   336	        if (rank == 0) {
   337	            counts.resize(size);
   338	            displacements.resize(size);
   339	            for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {
   340	                int first, rows;
   341	                rowPartition(n, size, r, first, rows);
   342	                counts[r] = rows * n;
   343	                displacements[r] = first * n;
   344	            }
   345	            global_energy.resize(static_cast<size_t>(n_elements));
   346	        }
   347	        MPI_Gatherv(world.energy.data(), local_count, MPI_DOUBLE,
   348	                    rank == 0 ? global_energy.data() : nullptr,
   349	                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
   350	                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
   351	                    MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   352	        if (rank == 0) print_results(global_energy, "ElementEnergy");
   353	    }
   354	
   355	    const bool valid = !validate || validateResults(world, rank);
   356	    MPI_Finalize();
   357	    return valid ? 0 : 1;
   358	}

+static inline void updateRows(World& world, int begin_row, int end_row) {

codex
Implemented unconditional distributed-memory MPI parallelism while preserving the `unstructured` executable name.

Key changes:

- Row-block mesh decomposition with balanced ownership.
- Per-rank storage for owned elements plus two halo rows.
- Nonblocking halo exchange overlapped with interior computation.
- Structure-of-arrays dynamic state to minimize communication.
- Distributed hash and validation reductions.
- Global gathering only when `-r` requests full output.
- Root-only reporting and maximum-rank timing.
- Required MPI linkage in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/unstructured_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_mpi_r4/unstructured/CMakeLists.txt:7).
- MPI implementation in [unstructured.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/unstructured_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_mpi_r4/unstructured/unstructured.cpp:52).

Verification completed:

- Clean CMake release build with MPI.
- Identical hashes and results across 1, 2, and 4 ranks.
- Matches the original serial reference hashes.
- Handles more ranks than mesh rows.
- Default benchmark: 23.873 ms on 1 rank versus 5.659 ms on 4 ranks in the test environment, with identical hash `DDF547FC297B5C00`.

+    const int base = n / size;
+    const int remainder = n % size;
+    count = base + (rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);

+static inline void updateRows(World& world, int begin_row, int end_row) {
+    const int n = world.grid_size;
+    for (int local_x = begin_row; local_x < end_row; ++local_x) {
+        const size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(local_x) * n;
+        const size_t end = begin + n;
+        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {
+            const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];
+            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem.material_idx];
+            const val_t this_energy = world.energy[i];
+            val_t flux = mat.external_flow;
+            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem.num_connections; ++j) {
+                flux += computeFlux(mat, this_energy, elem.connected_flux[j],
+                                    world.energy[elem.connected_idx[j]]);
+            }
+            world.energy_swap[i] = this_energy + flux;
+            world.total_flux_swap[i] = world.total_flux[i] + std::abs(flux);
+        }
+    }
+}

tokens used

Implemented unconditional distributed-memory MPI parallelism while preserving the `unstructured` executable name.

Key changes:

- Row-block mesh decomposition with balanced ownership.
- Per-rank storage for owned elements plus two halo rows.
- Nonblocking halo exchange overlapped with interior computation.
- Structure-of-arrays dynamic state to minimize communication.
- Distributed hash and validation reductions.
- Global gathering only when `-r` requests full output.
- Root-only reporting and maximum-rank timing.
- Required MPI linkage in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/unstructured_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_mpi_r4/unstructured/CMakeLists.txt:7).
- MPI implementation in [unstructured.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/unstructured_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_mpi_r4/unstructured/unstructured.cpp:52).

Verification completed:

- Clean CMake release build with MPI.
- Identical hashes and results across 1, 2, and 4 ranks.
- Matches the original serial reference hashes.
- Handles more ranks than mesh rows.
- Default benchmark: 23.873 ms on 1 rank versus 5.659 ms on 4 ranks in the test environment, with identical hash `DDF547FC297B5C00`.
